     Appointment, re-election and removal of Directors

     Each of the executive Directors, non-executive Directors and independent non-executive Directors has
     entered into a service contract or a letter of appointment with the Company for an initial term of three
     years commencing from the Listing Date, subject to renewal after expiry of the then current term. Such
     term is subject to his retirement by rotation and re-election at an annual general meeting of the Company
     in accordance with the Articles of Association. The Articles of Association provide that the Company may
     by ordinary resolution elect any person to be a Director either to fill a casual vacancy or as an addition
     to the existing Directors. Any Director so appointed shall hold office only until the next following annual
     general meeting and shall then be eligible for re-election at such meeting.

     In accordance with the Articles of Association, at each annual general meeting of the Company, one-third
     of the Directors for the time being shall retire from office by rotation provided that every Director shall be
     subject to retirement at an annual general meeting at least once every three years. The members of the
     Company may, at any general meetings convened and held in accordance with the Articles of Association,
     by ordinary resolution remove a Director at any time before the expiration of his period of office
     notwithstanding anything to the contrary in the Articles of Association or in any agreement between the
     Company and such Director (but without prejudice to any claim for damages under any such agreement).

     Compensation of Directors and Senior Management

     The emoluments of the Directors and Senior Management of the Group are decided by the Board with
     reference to the recommendation given by the Remuneration Committee, having regard to the Group’s
     operating results, individual performance and comparable market statistics.

     Details of Directors and the top five highest paid individuals are set out in notes 10 and 11 to the
     consolidated financial statements. During the Reporting Period, no emoluments were paid by the Group
     to any Directors or any of the five highest paid individuals as an inducement to join or upon joining
     the Group or as compensation for loss of office. For the year ended December 31, 2025, none of the
     Directors has waived or agreed to waive any emoluments.

     Except as disclosed above, no other payments have been made or are payable, for the year ended
     December 31, 2025, by the Group to or on behalf of any of the Directors.

     Directors ’ training and professional development

     Every newly appointed Director has been given a comprehensive, formal and tailored induction on
     appointment. Subsequently, the Directors will receive updates on the Listing Rules, legal and other
     regulatory requirements and the latest development of the Group’s business and are encouraged to
     participate in continuous professional development to develop their knowledge and skills.

     During the year ended December 31, 2025, the Directors were regularly briefed on the amendments to or
     updates on the relevant laws, rules and regulations. Internally-facilitated briefings for Directors would be
     arranged and reading material on relevant topics would be provided to Directors where appropriate. All
     Directors are encouraged to attend relevant training courses at the Company’s expense.

Board meetings

Code provision C.5.1 of Part 2 of the CG Code stipulates that Board meetings should be held at least four
times a year at approximately quarterly intervals with active participation of the majority of the Directors,
either in person or through electronic means of communications. Apart from regular Board meetings, the
Chairman should at least annually hold meeting with the independent non-executive Directors without the
presence of other Directors under the code provision C.2.7 of Part 2 of the CG Code.

During the year ended December 31, 2025, four Board meetings were held at which the Board
considered and approved interim results announcement, interim report, annual results announcement,
annual report and other business affairs of the Group. The Company expects to continue to convene at
least four regular meetings in each financial year at approximately quarterly intervals in accordance with
code provision C.5.1 of Part 2 of the CG Code.

     Board currently comprises three independent non-executive Directors and being one-third of the Board,

     potential candidates of independent non-executive Directors, the nomination committee will review
     their qualification, skills, knowledge, independent views and having regard to the nomination policy and
     the board diversity policy of the Company. Nomination committee also assessed the time commitment
     devoted by and independence of independent non-executive Directors annually. External independent
     professional advice is also available to all Directors (including independent non-executive Directors)
     whenever deemed necessary. During the year ended December 31, 2025, the Board reviewed and
     considered the implementation of above mechanisms were effective.

The Board has established three committees with specific written terms of reference to oversee particular

Audit Committee

The Company established the audit committee in compliance with Rules 3.21 to 3.23 of the Listing
Rules with written terms of reference in compliance with the Corporate Governance Code set forth in
Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules. The primary functions of the audit committee are to assist our Board by
providing an independent view of the effectiveness of the financial reporting process, internal control and
risk management systems of our Group, overseeing the audit process, and performing other duties and
responsibilities as assigned by our Board.

The audit committee consists of one non-executive Director, Mr. Qi CHEN, and two independent

     The Company established the remuneration committee in compliance with Rule 3.25 of the Listing Rules

     Rules with written terms of reference in compliance with Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules. The primary
     functions of the nomination committee include, without limitation, reviewing the structure, size and
     composition of our Board, assessing the independence of independent non-executive Directors and
     making recommendations to our Board on matters relating to the appointment of Directors. In identifying
     and selecting suitable candidates for directorships, the nomination committee would consider the
     candidate’s gender, skills, age, professional experience, knowledge, cultural, education background and
     other qualities. The ultimate decision of the appointment will be based on merit and the contribution
     which the selected candidates will bring to our Board. The Company has adopted nomination policy,
     which is incorporated in the terms of reference of the nomination committee and sets out the selection
     criteria and nomination procedures for identifying and recommending candidates for appointment or
     reappointment of Director.

     RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL

     The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management and internal control systems and
     reviewing their effectiveness in order to achieve the Company’s objectives. The Company adopted a
     series of internal control policies, measures, and procedures designed to provide reasonable assurance,
     which include effective standards, efficient operations, reliable financial reporting and compliance
     with applicable laws and regulations. The internal control system can only provide reasonable and not
     absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss, as they are designed to manage, rather than
     eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives. Below is a summary of the internal control
     policies, measures, and procedures we have implemented:

     •    The Company conducted, through an annual audit of the internal controls of each business
          department, a review on the effectiveness of the risk management and internal control systems for
          the year ended December 31, 2025 and considered them effective and adequate, with no material
          changes from the last annual review nor significant control failings or weakness identified. The
          internal audit included reviewing the management of financial statements, sales and receivables,
          purchasing and payment, fixed assets and intangible assets, human resource, research and
          development, nature and extent of significant risks (and the Company’s ability to respond to such
          risks and changes). The audit procedures could be summarized as below, including but not limited
          to:

          o    Interview with responsible personnel;

          o    Obtain and review the required documents;

          o    Test the design and operating effectiveness of the internal control system

     •    The Company published the risk management and internal control policies, measures and
          procedures to ensure that the Company maintained reasonable and effective internal controls and
          compliance with applicable laws and regulations. Besides, the Company insisted on monitoring the
          implementation of internal control policies, measures, and procedures, making sure that they were
          the most updated version based on the current business model.

     •    The Company implemented the relevant internal control policies, measures and procedures on
          the site and making quarterly and annual regular inspections about the on-site implementation
          of such policies, measures, and procedures for each stage of the Company’s drug discovery and
          development process.

     •    The Company adopted various measures and procedures regarding each aspect of the Company’s
          business operation, such as project management, quality assurance, environmental protection, and
          occupational health and safety. The Company provided the periodic training for the employees,
          which was one part of Employee Training Program. The Company also required the staff to carry out
          business activities in accordance with relevant laws, regulations and Company policies by regularly
          communicating updates and reminders through emails, staff meetings.

     •    The Company has developed internal policies that provide general guide to the Company’s
          Directors, officers, senior management and relevant employees in handling confidential information,
          monitoring information disclosure and responding to enquiries. Control procedures have been
          implemented to prevent unauthorized access and use of inside information.
                                                           2025 ANNUAL REPORT     KEYMED BIOSCIENCES INC.       49




                                                                   Corporate Governance Report




•    The Company has also developed a risk management process to identify, evaluate and manage
     significant risks and to resolve material internal control defects. Senior management of the Group
     is responsible for the risk reporting process. Risks identified are documented and mitigation plans
     are devised. The risk assessment is reviewed by certain members of the senior management and
     presented to the Audit Committee and the Board for their review.

•    The audit committee had the responsibility for monitoring the effectiveness of the risk management
     and internal control systems. It is willing to take in achieving the Company’s strategic objectives,
     and establishing and maintaining appropriate and effective internal control systems.

SHAREHOLDERS ’ RIGHTS

Convening an extraordinary general meeting

Pursuant to Article 12.3 of the Articles of Association, the Board may, whenever it thinks fit, convene an
extraordinary general meeting. Extraordinary general meetings shall also be convened on the requisition
of one or more Shareholders holding, at the date of deposit of the requisition, not less than one tenth of
the paid up capital of the Company having the right of voting at general meetings. Such requisition shall
be made in writing to the Board or the Secretary for the purpose of requiring an extraordinary general
meeting to be called by the Board for the transaction of any business specified in such requisition.
Such meeting shall be held within two calendar months after the deposit of such requisition. If within 21
days of such deposit, the Board fails to proceed to convene such meeting, the requisitionist(s) himself
(themselves) may do so in the same manner, and all reasonable expenses incurred by the requisitionist(s)
as a result of the failure of the Board shall be reimbursed to the requisitionist(s) by the Company.

       Environmental, Social and Governance Report


ABOUT THE REPORT

Keymed Biosciences Inc. (the “Company”, “Company” or “Keymed”) is pleased to release the 2025
Environmental, Social and Governance (“ESG”) Report (“ESG Report” or “this Report”), which is designed
to report on the Company’s implementation of sustainable development concepts and ESG practices
in 2025, and to respond to key ESG issues of concern to stakeholders. This Report should be read in
conjunction with the “Corporate Governance Report” section of the 2025 Annual Report of Keymed
Biosciences Inc. (the “2025 Annual Report”) to gain a more comprehensive understanding of the
Company’s ESG performance.

Scope of the Report

Unless otherwise stated, this Report covers the Company and all its subsidiaries. The time frame of this
Report is from January 1, 2025 to December 31, 2025 (the “Reporting Period”).

Preparation Standards of the Report

This Report has been prepared in accordance with the Environmental, Social and Governance Reporting
Code (the “ESG Reporting Code”) set out in Appendix C2 of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities
on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”) issued by The Stock Exchange of
Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”). It also references international guidelines such as IFRS S1
– General Requirements for Sustainability Related Financial Disclosures, IFRS S2 – Climate-Related
Disclosures , and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board (“SASB”) Standards. This Report
has complied with the “mandatory disclosure” and “comply or explain” provisions outlined in the
ESG Reporting Code, and taken the four reporting principles of materiality, quantitative, balance and
consistency as the basis of preparation.

Reporting principles   Application

Materiality            During the Reporting Period, the Company identified the ESG-related materiality
                       issues through the materiality issue assessment, and reviewed and confirmed
                       the ESG-related materiality issues, so as to primarily respond to and disclose the
                       identified materiality issues. Please refer to the sub-section headed “Materiality
                       Issue Assessment” for details.

Quantitative           The Company establishes a data statistics mechanism for the measurable key
                       performance indicators specified in the ESG Reporting Code and discloses
                       the calculation results, and specifies the standards, methods and applicable
                       assumptions adopted in this Report.

Balance                This Report reflects objective facts, disclosing both positive and negative
                       indicators.

Consistency            The Company adopts consistent statistical methodologies with the previous
                       ESG report and has made the necessary explanations for any changes in such
                       methodologies.
52   KEYMED BIOSCIENCES INC.    2025 ANNUAL REPORT




     Environmental, Social and Governance Report

     records, misleading statements or material omissions and is willing to bear responsibility for the
     authenticity, accuracy and completeness of its contents.

     Access to this Report

     ESG MANAGEMENT

     Board Statement

     The Board, as the highest decision-making body of the Company’s ESG work, bears overall
     responsibility for ESG strategies and reporting. The Board is responsible for supervising and approving
     ESG management policies and strategies related to the Company’s business development, regularly
     considering their implementation, and periodically reviewing the progress of the implementation of the
     ESG related targets; also reviewing the results of ESG issues assessments and prioritization (including
     ESG issues and ESG-related risks), and continuously supervising the implementation of ESG management
     as well as the matters that may cause significant impacts and risks to the Company. The Board authorizes
     the ESG Working Group to carry out the Company’s ESG related matters, aiming to integrate sustainable
     development concepts covering environmental, social and governance aspects into all aspects of the
     Company’s business operation and development, so as to comprehensively enhance the Company’s ESG
     performance.

     ESG Governance Structure

     The Company has established a clear ESG governance structure with clearly defined rights and
     responsibilities to thoroughly implement the concept of sustainable development. The Board, as the
     highest decision-making and responsible body for ESG work, has authorized the establishment of an
     ESG Working Group as a specialized coordination and execution body, and has formulated the Terms
     of Reference of the ESG Working Group to clarify the responsibilities of each level regarding ESG-
     related matters. By establishing a three-tier management system of “Board – ESG Working Group –
     departments, subsidiaries and branches”, the Company achieves coordinated management and efficient
     implementation of ESG matters, continuously enhancing the systematic and professional level of ESG
     management.

MATERIALITY ISSUE ASSESSMENT

In order to effectively review the focus areas of ESG management and to gain an in-depth understanding
of the concerns and expectations of stakeholders on the Company’s ESG issues, we conducted an issue
assessment based on the principle of “materiality”.

During the Reporting Period, with reference to ESG guidelines such as the Stock Exchange’s ESG
Reporting Code , the IFRS Sustainability Disclosure Standards and SASB standards, and taking into
account the Company’s development strategy, business characteristics, and the development trend of
the pharmaceutical industry, the Company systematically identified, assessed, and reviewed ESG issues
from two dimensions of “importance to the enterprise” and “importance to stakeholders”, while also
considering the “financial importance” of the issues, ultimately determining the material issues for the
Reporting Period.


                        Step 1: Update the database of materiality issues
                            Update the database of materiality issues by confirming
                        the previous ESG issues and supplementing the new ESG issues




                               Step 2: Identify and screen ESG issues

                       Identify and screen ESG issues with high relevance to the Company




                                  Step 3: Evaluate materiality issues
          Evaluate the materiality of ESG issues, resulting in the outcome of ESG materiality issues
          from two dimensions of the “importance to the enterprise” and the “importance to
                       stakeholders” and taking into account of "financial importance"




                                       Step 4: Review and confirm

         Formulate a list of materiality issues of this year after the review and confirmation by the Board


                                  Flowchart of Materiality Issue Assessment

     Environmental, Social and Governance Report




     Based on the aforementioned materiality assessment, the Company ultimately identified 20 material
     issues. Among them, two new issues, namely “R&D Ethics” and “Inclusive Healthcare”, were added
     during the Reporting Period, and the importance of pharmacovigilance and other aspects within the
     “Product Quality and Safety” issue was further clarified. This Report will focus on these issues and
     comply with relevant disclosure requirements such as the Stock Exchange’s ESG Reporting Code ,
     providing a detailed response to the Company’s ESG management practices and performance during the

INTEGRITY AND COMPLIANCE, STEADFAST PRACTICE LEADS TO FAR-REACHING SUCCESS

Keymed is committed to navigating its business with high-standard governance, striving for steady and
sustainable long-term development. It internalizes compliant operations as a management principle,
adheres to business ethics, and strengthens its anti-corruption defenses. Simultaneously, it places
great emphasis on information security and privacy protection, and treats intellectual property as a core
strategic asset for full lifecycle management, thereby building a solid protective barrier for innovative
achievements and core competitiveness.

Compliance Operation

Keymed strictly adheres to the Company Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國公
司法》) , the Securities Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國證券法》) , and other
relevant laws and regulations. It has formulated and implemented risk management and internal control
policies, measures, and procedures such as the Keymed Due Diligence Process ( 《康諾亞合規盡職調查
流程》) , ensuring that the Company maintains reasonable and effective internal controls and complies
with applicable laws and regulations. During the Reporting Period, the Company continued to optimize
core management systems such as the External Activities and Conduct Compliance Guidelines (《對外活
動和行為合規指引》) , ensuring the continuous and effective operation of the internal control system and
promoting the Company’s lawful, compliant, orderly and efficient development.

The Company has established risk management procedures to identify, assess, and manage significant
risks (including ESG-related risks), and to address major internal control deficiencies. During the
Reporting Period, the Company updated its supplier and partner risk management procedures to address
major strategic risks (such as geopolitical conflicts and disruptions in key supply chains), standardized
and tightened due diligence, ongoing monitoring, and exit management processes for key third parties;
formulated and implemented the Pharmaceutical Representative Behavior Management Policy (      《醫藥代表
行為管理政策》) , established a systematic risk assessment mechanism for pharmaceutical representatives’
academic promotion activities, and enhanced the compliance control effectiveness of academic
promotion activities.

The Company continues to promote the construction of the compliance system, and has established a
more comprehensive pre-operational and post-operational internal supervision system, which has formed
the three lines of defence (i.e. the business departments – compliance and finance department – internal
audit department) to implement risk management in their respective positions.

     Business Ethics

     Keymed has always insisted on the highest standards of commercial ethics and strictly complied with
     laws and regulations on the prevention of bribery, extortion, fraud and money laundering, such as the
     Criminal Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國刑法》) , the Law of the People’s Republic
     of China Against Unfair Competition (《中華人民共和國反不正當競爭法》) , and the Anti-Money Laundering
     Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國反洗錢法》) . The Company has formulated
     rules and regulations, including the Anti-fraud Management Measures (《反舞弊管理辦法》) , the Anti-
     corruption and Anti-bribery Policies (《反腐敗和反賄賂政策》) , and the Fault Liability Regulations (《過
     錯追究責任辦法》) , and has requested employees to sign the Anti-fraud and Anti-bribery Commitment
     Letter (《反舞弊反商業賄賂承諾書》) . The Company continues to improve its anti-corruption and business
     ethics system. During the Reporting Period, it issued and implemented the Policy on Brand Reminders,
     Marketing Items, Medical Education Supplies and Customary Gifts (《品牌提示物、推廣輔助用品、醫療教
     育用品及風俗禮品政策》) , which defines the compliance scope and usage purposes of gifts and items in
     business interactions, regulates interactions with suppliers, partners, and other business associates, and
     strengthens prior reporting and approval as well as management supervision responsibilities to effectively
     prevent business ethics risks.

     The Company regularly conducts anti-fraud audits, and conducted investigation into possible non-
     compliance with laws and regulations to identify and correct possible problems in order to strengthen the
     risk control of fraud and commercial bribery. During the Reporting Period, Keymed was not aware of any
     non-compliance of the Company or its employees in relation to corruption, extortion, fraud and money
     laundering.

     The Company has established and publicized complaint and reporting channels, standardizing the
     reporting process, scope, investigation and handling procedures in accordance with management
     documents such as the Anti-fraud Management Measures . Any form of fraudulent behavior by
     management and employees, as well as violations of company policies, regulations, and compliance
     ethics guidelines, are included in the scope of reporting. The Company also encouraged its employees,
     customers and suppliers to actively supervise through integrity commitment letters in business contracts,
     communication with suppliers via email, training at all levels, and displaying reporting channels in places
     such as construction sites. In addition, the Company implemented measures to protect whistleblowers
     to keep their identity and relevant information in strict confidence, thereby effectively protecting the
     personal safety and legal rights of whistleblowers.

     Keymed actively promoted integrity culture and has conducted anti-corruption training and work integrity
     education training for both Directors and employees. During the Reporting Period, the Company provided
     several trainings on the rules and regulations of the Anti-corruption and Anti-commercial Bribery ( 《反
     腐敗反商業賄賂》) , trade secret protection and sales compliance, and organized offline and online
     examinations after the trainings to ensure that the employees fully understood the training content so as
     to enhance their anti-corruption awareness.

INFORMATION SECURITY AND PRIVACY PROTECTION

In strict compliance with relevant laws and regulations of the state and the places where the Company
operates on information security and privacy protection, such as the Cybersecurity Law of the People’s
Republic of China (《中華人民共和國網絡安全法》) , the Data Security Law of the People’s Republic of
China (《中華人民共和國數據安全法》) , Keymed has worked out a series of systems and regulations on
data management and information backup to ensure the safe and stable operation of the Company’s
networks. The Company has established an information technology (“IT”) department, the Data Protection
and Privacy Office, which is responsible for coordinating the Company’s privacy compliance matters,
promoting communication and training of relevant systems and ensuring that employees fully understand
and strictly comply with relevant laws and regulations, thereby protecting the information security and
privacy of customers and users.

Keymed is committed to enhancing information security protection and continuously improving the
Standard Operating Procedures (“SOP”) related to information security. The Company has adopted an
electronic data collection system to realize information desensitization feedback to protect sensitive data.
At the same time, it has classified confidential information and specified the scope, authority and use
procedures of information under different categories. In addition, the Company also adopted the following
information safety management measures to enhance information security protection:

•    New employees are required to sign commitment documents related to the use of office computers
     and data integrity during the onboarding process;

•    Providing continuous training on information security and data integrity to all employees, covering
     topics such as the security of employees’ passwords, proper use of computers, email and removable
     storage media and phishing telephone or email identification;
62   KEYMED BIOSCIENCES INC.   2025 ANNUAL REPORT




     Environmental, Social and Governance Report




     •   For external suppliers, we implement centralized control and the principle of minimum
         authorization, limiting their access to information resources through network segregation;

     •   Using the threat detection system to conduct security audit on network traffic and established
         abnormal traffic analysis and warning mechanism to realize threat control;

     •   Implementing access control through Network Policy Server (NPS) admission authentication for
         wireless networks. Guest wireless access is only permitted for internet connectivity, while access to
         the internal network is prohibited, thereby further reducing information security risks;

     •   Filtering and controlling viruses and phishing emails through the email security gateway.

Keymed clearly defines the scope of confidential information and the corresponding management
methods to ensure the security of trade secrets and other confidential information. The Company has
signed confidentiality agreements with its employees and partners, strictly regulating the data usage
permissions in various processes, and promises not to share data with external third parties, effectively
preventing the risk of information and privacy leakage. At the same time, the Company also adopts the
following privacy protection measures:

•    Prior consent: The Company undertook to protect the privacy of patients who participate in clinical
     trials. Before the patients were enrolled in a clinical trial, the relevant personnel would inform the
     patients of the Company’s data privacy and protection policies and measures. The Company would
     collect relevant clinical data only with the consent of the enrolled patients;

•    De-identification: The Company strictly conducted de-identification protection on the patient
     information by deleting the personal identifiers of the enrolled patients, including name, telephone
     number, address, ID number and other information that could be used to identify the patients when
     generating clinical trial data;

•    Data storage and access: The Company committed not to disclose patient information to unrelated
     parties. The personal information of the enrolled patients was kept at the medical institutions which
     have signed internal control protocols to restrict and monitor access to the data. Confidential patient
     data was only available to authorized employees.

     Intellectual Property Protection

     Keymed has always regarded intellectual property as a core strategic asset and an important cornerstone
     of ESG governance. It strictly adheres to laws and regulations such as the Patent Law of the People’s
     Republic of China (《中華人民共和國專利法》) , the Trademark Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中
     華人民共和國商標法》) , and the Copyright Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國著作權
     法》) . It has established and continuously improved internal management systems for intellectual property
     protection, including the System for Infringement Prevention (   《防侵權制度》) , the System for Research
     and Development Achievement Protection (《研發成果保護制度》) and the System for Management of
     Scientific and Technological Achievements of Research and Development Center (《研發中心科技成果管
     理制度》) . It fully integrates intellectual property management into the entire process from R&D project
     initiation to product launch, continuously strengthening intellectual property protection efforts.

     Keymed continues to strengthen the full-process management of intellectual property, effectively
     safeguarding the innovative achievements transformed from R&D investment. It persistently enhances the
     cultivation of intellectual property literacy among all employees, focusing on improving their awareness
     of protection, risk prevention and control capabilities, and the mindset of achievement transformation.
     Additionally, it introduces new technological means such as artificial intelligence to enhance the
     efficiency of patent retrieval and risk assessment, thereby further consolidating the Company’s core
     competitiveness in intellectual property in the field of biomedicine.

     Craftsmanship Builds Quality, Innovation Ensures Safety

     Product Quality

Quality Management System

Adhering to the concept of high standard of quality management, Keymed established and effectively
operated a quality management system in compliance with the requirements of the Good Manufacturing
Practice of Medical Products (“GMP”), the World Health Organization (WHO) and the Quality Risk
Management (《質量風險管理》) guidelines (ICHQ9) and the Pharmaceutical Quality System (《藥品
品質體系》) (ICHQ10) of the International Conference on Harmonization of Technical Requirements
for Registration of Pharmaceuticals for Human Use, and produced marketed drugs that meet drug
registration requirements in accordance with validated and approved processes. The Company has clearly
defined the quality management workflow and standards at each level in accordance with the quality
management system and divided the duties among the persons in charge at each level to better promote
the daily product quality management and ensure the effectiveness of the quality management system.

Quality Control and Assurance

The Company strictly controlled the quality in each segment, and implemented targeted corrective actions
and preventive actions (CAPA) for deviations, changes, and other situations during production, thereby
achieving full life cycle coverage of products R&D, registration, testing and production. On this basis, the
Company adheres to full-process quality supervision for each batch of products, encompassing raw and
auxiliary material inspection upon arrival, intermediate product process control, real-time monitoring of
production steps, finished product release inspection, and long-term stability research, which achieves
full-process quality control from the source to the end.

To ensure the implementation of the aforementioned management requirements, all production bases
of Keymed that have been put into operation have established independent quality control laboratories,
equipped with experienced professional testing teams and first-class precision testing equipment, capable
of conducting comprehensive inspections of materials and products. As of the end of the Reporting
Period, Keymed’s laboratory testing capabilities have fully covered key areas such as instrumental
analysis, biochemical and physicochemical testing, and microbiological testing. These capabilities not
only comply with strict domestic and international drug quality regulations but also fully meet the testing
needs of the Company’s diversified products, providing a solid foundation for quality control at all stages
of drug research and development, production, and post-market launch.

The Company places great emphasis on the safety of products during storage and transportation. We
have formulated normative documents such as the Finished Product Shipping Procedures (      《成品發運
規程》) , the Rules on Administration of the Allocation of Materials and Supplies (《物料和物資調撥管理
規程》) , and the Rules on the Administration of Product Warehousing, Storage and Distribution ( 《產品入
庫、儲存、發放管理規程》) , strictly regulating operational processes such as warehousing, distribution,
and allocation to ensure that drug storage meets requirements. For drugs transported via cold chain,
the Company works closely with transportation suppliers to conduct cold chain transport verification
and adopts verified and qualified transportation methods. We monitor temperature throughout the entire
transportation process, ensuring temperature control and traceability from factory to customer, thereby
guaranteeing patient medication safety and supply continuity.

In addition, Keymed adheres to strict quality certification standards and regularly conducts internal
quality management reviews and self-inspections. The Company conducts at least one comprehensive
audit and review of the factory’s GMP quality management system and product quality each year,
covering key systems such as quality management, production management, plant facilities and
equipment, material management, packaging, and labeling systems, in order to continuously improve the
production quality management system. During the Reporting Period, 100% of the Company’s production
bases that have been put into commercial operation in China passed the GMP inspection.

     Quality Risk Management

     Keymed has established a quality risk management system in accordance with relevant requirements,
     utilizing risk management methods and tools to identify, assess, control, communicate, and review quality
     risks throughout the entire product lifecycle. Based on risk considerations, effective decisions are made
     to eliminate, reduce, or control potential risks, thereby effectively safeguarding patient interests.

     Product Recall

     The Company complied with the relevant provisions of the drug regulatory authorities, and formulated
     the Product Recall Management Procedures (《產品召回管理程序》) , the Nonconformity Management
     Procedures (《不合格管理程序》) and the Sales Return Management Procedure (《銷售退貨管理程序》) and
     other product recall related management methods. For products that involved in quality issues such as
     product complaints and adverse reactions, the Company took necessary recall measures upon request
     and recorded and destroyed the products returned due to quality. During the Reporting Period, no
     products of the Company were required to be recalled for safety and health reasons.

     Quality Culture Construction

     The Company has established a comprehensive training management standard, assigning dedicated
     personnel to oversee training related to quality management, ensuring that our team possesses solid
     professional knowledge and skills. To enhance employees’ quality awareness and guarantee product
     quality, the Company conducts various quality management trainings, including annual training (quality
     compliance, risk prevention and control), pre-effective document training (ensuring the implementation
     of the quality system), and on-the-job training. Furthermore, we train personnel in GMP-related positions
     through various aspects such as GMP standards, quality management processes, and key job operations,
     ensuring they possess the necessary qualifications to carry out GMP activities in their respective
     positions.

     Pharmacovigilance

      Keymed strictly adheres to laws and regulations such as the Good Pharmacovigilance Practice (     《藥物
      警戒質量管理規範》) and the Measures for the Reporting and Monitoring of Adverse Drug Reactions
     (《藥品不良反應報告和監測管理辦法》) . It has formulated and implemented the Main Document of
      the Pharmacovigilance System (《藥物警戒體系主文件》) as well as supporting management systems
      and operational procedures. Relying on its internal monitoring system and product safety policies, it
      systematically and normatively carries out various pharmacovigilance tasks, fully ensuring the medication
      safety of patients.

     The Company has established a sound pharmacovigilance management system and set up a Drug Safety
     Committee as the core decision-making body for pharmacovigilance work, responsible for decision-
     making on risk assessment, incident handling and risk control. The committee comprises core leaders
     from multiple departments to achieve cross-departmental collaboration and coordination. Additionally,
     an executive team consisting of senior pharmacovigilance personnel has been established, forming a
     “decision-making – execution” closed-loop management to ensure efficient and orderly operation of
     pharmacovigilance work.
                                                            2025 ANNUAL REPORT      KEYMED BIOSCIENCES INC.        67




                                            Environmental, Social and Governance Report




The Company carries out full-life cycle drug safety management, integrating the concept of risk
prevention and control throughout the entire process of drug design and screening, clinical trials, and
post-marketing surveillance. Before drug launch, our pharmacovigilance team is deeply involved in
clinical trials, comprehensively assessing potential safety risks based on drug mechanism of action, non-
clinical pharmacokinetic, pharmacodynamic, and toxicological data, and fully considering the target
prescription population and special population in the clinical protocol design. We also cooperate with the
data security monitoring committee to regularly review data quality, promptly identify new safety signals
during the clinical trial stage, and conduct analysis and evaluation. After drug launch, the Company
quickly integrates the safety data from the clinical trial stage to establish a closed-loop management of
“monitoring – identification – evaluation – control”, ensuring early detection, early assessment and early
disposal of drug safety risks, thereby minimizing the medication risks for patients.

The Company has established a four-in-one adverse reaction information collection network encompassing
“Company, medical institutions, distributors and patients”, and set up an intelligent pharmacovigilance
information management platform. By integrating domestic and international adverse reaction monitoring
data, literature data, and clinical usage data, we achieve the collection, rapid reporting, analysis,
and signal mining of suspected drug adverse reaction information. We empower pharmacovigilance
work with intelligent technology to enhance risk early warning and response efficiency. The Company
has also established smooth information feedback channels, guiding medical institutions to actively
report, distributors to assist in collection, and patients to voluntarily provide feedback. Furthermore, we
strengthen communication and coordination with national and local adverse drug reaction monitoring
centers, promptly respond to regulatory requirements, and actively cooperate with regulatory authorities
in carrying out risk investigations and special rectification work.

Meanwhile, Keymed has been continuously conducting training on pharmacovigilance and safety
information reporting, covering all employees and key personnel, to continuously enhance their risk
awareness and professional capabilities, providing a solid foundation for the effective operation of the
pharmacovigilance system.

•    Training for all staff: Clarify the product safety information reporting responsibilities of employees
     and partners (medical institutions, distributors, etc.), ensuring timely feedback of safety information;

•    Pharmacovigilance personnel:

     –     Induction training: Develop a systematic induction training plan to help new employees quickly
           adapt to job requirements;

     –     On-the-job training: Continuously conduct specialized training on regulatory processes, safety
           incident assessment, risk management plans, and other related topics to ensure the ability to
           perform duties;

•    Training for clinical trial project personnel: Conduct targeted training for all personnel involved in
     clinical trial projects, covering the scope of safety events to be reported in the clinical trial protocol,
     as well as the information reporting channels and timeframes specified in the safety management
     plan.

     Customer Service

     Keymed attaches great importance to the opinions of its customers and has formulated and continuously
     improved management measures such as the Product Quality Complaint Management Procedure (        《產品
     質量投訴管理規程》) , clarifying the complaint handling process and management mechanism to ensure
     that complaints from customers are handled in a timely and proper manner. During the Reporting Period,
     the Company further improved the Quality Complaint Handling Management Procedure (《質量投訴處理
     管理程序》) , perfecting the process of receiving and investigating quality complaints, and stipulating the
     implementation of complaint investigation and handling exceeding the deadline, thereby standardizing the
     complaint handling process. In the event of a product complaint, the Company will immediately conduct
     an investigation to identify potential product safety hazards, analyze the reasons for the complaint
     accurately, so as to formulate and implement solutions and remedial measures, and promptly respond to
     the customer. During the Reporting Period, the Company has not received any complaints regarding its
     products or service quality and safety.

     R&D and Innovation

     Keymed remains committed to driving high-quality corporate development through technological
     innovation, continuously enhancing its independent R&D capabilities and accelerating the clinical
     development of multiple innovative products. The Company has continuously refined its R&D system
     and management model, established a highly integrated R&D platform that focuses on the fields of
     immunology and oncology, thereby achieving comprehensive synergy from early discovery through clinical
     development. The Company’s core proprietary platforms include the antibody discovery platform, the
     KeyMedSTAR™ (Keymed Superior Topo1i ADC Reagents) ADC Platform, the TCE Bispecific Antibodies
     Platform, the VESIR™ (VEhicle for siRNA delivery) oligonucleotide platform, the small molecule platform
     and the KeyCND™ (Keymed Central Nervous System Delivery) Blood-Brain Barrier-Penetrating Antibody
     Delivery Platform.

     To enhance R&D efficiency, Keymed has established an integrated R&D system covering target validation,
     lead molecule discovery and optimization, preclinical evaluation, process development, translational
     research, clinical development and manufacturing. This system enables the Company to continuously
     build and advance a diversified pipeline of differentiated innovative therapies, including monoclonal
     antibodies, bispecific antibodies, antibody drug conjugates and oligonucleotide drugs in an efficient and
     cost-effective manner. As of the end of the Reporting Period, the Company has applied for a total of 249
     patents and obtained 35 patent authorizations, representing an 84% increase compared to the previous
     year.

 Case: Keymed, together with its core product Kangyueda (康悅達 ®) (Stapokibart), was jointly listed on
 the “China’s Innovative Drug Decade Glory List”

 On September 24, 2025, the 2025 China Healthcare Decision-Makers Conference (2025CHDC) and the
 China Innovative Drugs Decade Achievement Retrospective Event was held in Beijing. The conference
 unveiled the highly anticipated “China’s Innovative Drug Decade Glory” list, where the Company and
 its core innovative product, Kangyueda (康悅達 ®), received prestigious awards of the “Industry-Leading
 Biotech Company” and “Industry-Leading Innovative Drug”, respectively. This “double recognition”
 is a high accolade from the industry, acknowledging the Company’s comprehensive strength and the
 exceptional value of its products.

     R&D Ethics

     Keymed always adheres to the ethical bottom line in R&D. In the process of continuously promoting drug
     innovation, it strictly follows domestic and international regulations and ethical guidelines, integrating
     the protection of clinical subjects and the welfare of experimental animals throughout the entire R&D
     process.

     Rights and interests of clinical subjects

     Keymed strictly adheres to laws and regulations such as the Civil Code of the People’s Republic of
     China (《中華人民共和國民法典》) , the Drug Administration Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中
     華人民共和國藥品管理法》) , and the Good Clinical Practice (《藥物臨床實驗質量管理規範》) , and abides
     by the international ethical principles of the Declaration of Helsinki . It conducts clinical application and
     trials in accordance with regulations such as the Measures for Ethical Review of Human Life Sciences
     and Medical Research Involving Humans (《涉及人的生命科學和醫學研究倫理審查辦法》) , effectively
     safeguarding the right to know and the right to free choice of clinical subjects.

     The Company places great emphasis on safeguarding the rights and interests of subjects. Before
     conducting clinical trials, the Company engages in thorough and objective communication with subjects
     to ensure they fully understand the potential risks, side effects, and potential benefits of the trial, as
     well as provides sufficient time for subjects to weigh their options and make an independent decision on
     whether to participate. All clinical trials can only commence after subjects have fully understood the trial
     protocol and signed the Informed Consent Form. During the trial, subjects are free to withdraw from the
     study at any time for any reason. If there are changes to the clinical research protocol or the investigator’s
     brochure, the Company will promptly update the Informed Consent Form, submit the revised materials to
     the ethics committee for approval, and inform subjects of the relevant changes as soon as possible, fully
     safeguarding the safety and rights of subjects.

     Animal Welfare

     Keymed strictly adheres to laws and regulations such as the General Code of Animal Welfare (  《實驗動
     物福利通則》) and the Laboratory Animal – Guideline for Ethical Review of Animal Welfare (《實驗動物福
     利倫理審查指南》) , and has established a Laboratory Animal Ethics Committee to handle matters related
     to laboratory animal ethics. The Company has formulated relevant documents such as the Laboratory
     Animal Welfare Ethical Review Procedures and Standard Operating Procedures for Laboratory Animals,
     and regularly conducts training on animal welfare to enhance the experimental operators’ understanding
     and implementation of animal welfare ethics, ensuring that animal experiments are conducted legally and
     in compliance.

     Before conducting animal experiments, we strictly adhere to the 3R principles of “reduction, replacement
     and refinement”. All experimental protocols must undergo rigorous review by the Experimental Animal
     Ethics Committee. During the experiment, animals are kept and managed in strict accordance with the
     standards set out in the Environment and Facilities for Experimental Animals (
                                                                                 《實驗動物環境與設施》) (GB
     14925-2023). Real-time dynamic monitoring of various physiological states of the animals is conducted,
     and clear criteria for determining humane endpoints are established. Euthanasia is promptly implemented
     when the endpoints are reached, to minimize animal suffering to the greatest extent possible.
                                                         2025 ANNUAL REPORT     KEYMED BIOSCIENCES INC.      71




                                          Environmental, Social and Governance Report




Supply Chain Management

 Keymed strives to build a sustainable supply chain and continuously improve the supplier management
 system. The Company has formulated documents such as the Procurement Management Regulations (    《採
 購管理規程》) and the Supplier Management Regulations (《供應商管理規程》) , to strictly control the entire
 process of supplier selection, access, evaluation and audit. For precursor chemicals, the Company has
 formulated the Procedures for Procurement, Filing, Warehousing and Disposal of Precursor Chemicals
(《易製毒化學品採購、備案、入庫、核銷規程》) , strictly verifying the legal business qualifications of
 suppliers to ensure that the entire process of procurement and use complies with the requirements of
 the Regulations on the Administration of Precursor Chemicals (《易製毒化學品管理條例》) . During the
 Reporting Period, the Company further strengthened supplier management, enhanced on-site audit
 requirements for material suppliers, and introduced a dynamic audit mechanism to continuously improve
 the compliance and sustainable management level of the supply chain.

The procurement category of the Company mainly included the third-party contracting services related
to preclinical evaluation and clinical trials of candidate drugs, raw materials, consumable materials,
machinery and equipment. As of the end of the Reporting Period, the Company had a total of 685
suppliers, all of which were managed and controlled in accordance with the Company ’s supplier
management regulations.

In terms of supplier access, Keymed has formulated the supplier access requirements and procedures,
formulated detailed access qualification guidelines for reagent chemicals and hazardous chemicals, and
reviewed the operation, production-related qualifications, management system and production standards
of suppliers. The Company has also incorporated ESG factors such as environmental management
qualification certification and quality management system certification into the review system, so as to
effectively manage risks such as environmental and social risks of suppliers. In particular, the Company
conducted regular on-site inspection of clinical quality to ensure that the key elements were legal and
compliant and met the access standards, and reviewed commercial ethics related factors to ensure
that the suppliers were free from administrative penalties, administrative litigation and tax evasion. The
Company also promoted green procurement, and from procurement of raw materials, cold chain logistics
to packaging materials, gave priority to energy conservation and environmental protection products.
Subject to factors such as quality and safety, the Company also procured second-hand equipment to
respond to the call for recycling economy. In addition, the Company promoted the alternative plan of
domestic equipment and consumables and cooperated strategically with local manufacturers to enhance
the resilience of the supply chain.

Keymed carried out the supplier evaluation in strict accordance with the Supplier Questionnaire , Quality
Assurance Agreement , Supplier Quality Problem Feedback Form , and other documents. The Company
conducted periodic and systematic evaluation on the supplier’s qualifications, technical capabilities,
compliance of response to user requirement description, and other indicators, with focus on key elements
such as cost, time, quality, and continuity.

Employment Management

Keymed strictly complies with the labor-related laws and regulations such as the Labor Law of the
People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國勞動法》) , the Labor Contract Law of the People’s Republic
of China (《中華人民共和國勞動合同法》) , the Social Insurance Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中
華人民共和國社會保險法》) , and the Provisions on the Prohibition of Using Child Labor (《禁止使用童工規
定》) , and continues to improve the human resources management systems in relation to remuneration
and dismissal, recruitment and promotion, working hours, holidays, equal opportunities, diversity,
anti-discrimination, other benefits and welfare, and prevention of child and forced labor, such as the
Employee Handbook .

Keymed has established a comprehensive recruitment system, formulated the Recruitment Management
System (《招聘管理制度》) , and strived to broaden the recruitment channels to recruit talents through
various channels such as online recruitment, campus recruitment and internal recommendations. At
the same time, the Company actively promotes equal employment and maintains an open, fair and
impartial recruitment mechanism to strive to eliminate discrimination based on gender, religion, race,
and other factors, so as to ensure that every employee of the Company can work in an environment of
equality. At Keymed, all employees join the workforce voluntarily, with on boarding procedures conducted
in accordance with internal procedures, ensuring legal compliance through verification of employee
information. Child labor and any form of forced labor are strictly prohibited. During the employment
period, the Company enters into labor contracts with its employees in accordance with the laws and
regulations to specify matters such as staff remuneration and benefits, position, dismissal, and reasons
for contract termination.

Keymed adheres to the principles of distribution according to work and equal pay for equal work. Every
year, it assesses salary levels based on market conditions, job positions, and other factors, dynamically
adjusts the salary structure, and provides fair and competitive compensation packages to attract
and retain outstanding talents. The Company also establishes a welfare system to provide mandated
benefits such as social insurance (including pension schemes, medical insurance, work-related injury
insurance, unemployment insurance and maternity insurance) and housing provident fund contributions,
and additional benefits such as performance-based bonuses, various subsidies and festive gifts. The
Company has purchased accident insurance for all employees, including interns, and has purchased
supplementary medical insurance for all employees in Chinese mainland. Furthermore, the Company
has opened a group-buying channel of commercial medical insurance for families of employees, so as to
provide better health protection for employees and their families. The Company also applied for high-level
talent subsidies from the national, provincial, municipal and district levels for qualified excellent talents
to motivate the employees.

The Company complies with the Labor Law of the People’s Republic of China on the eight-hour working
hours and has formulated reasonable working and rest time arrangements, and all employees are entitled
to paid holidays, statutory holidays, marriage leave, bereavement leave, maternity leave and other leave
rights.

The Company complies with international standards, such as the International Covenants of Human
Rights and the Universal Declaration of Human Rights , to improve the rules and standards of employment
and establish a mechanism for handling employment violations (such as the use of child labor or forced
labor), and organizes regular self-inspection and check of employment. In the event of non-compliance,
the Company will immediately conduct an investigation, take remedial measures, and deal with the matter
in accordance with the relevant laws and regulations to eliminate the recurrence of the incident.

     Occupational Health and Safety

     Keymed actively fulfills its primary responsibility for safe production and ensures the occupational health
     and safety of its employees. It has strictly abided by the Law of the People’s Republic of China on Work
     Safety (《中華人民共和國安全生產法》) , the Law of the People’s Republic of China on Prevention and
     Control of Occupational Diseases (《中華人民共和國職業病防治法》) , the Provisions on the Supervision
     and Administration of Occupational Health at Work Sites (《工作場所職業衛生監督管理規定》) , and other
     laws and regulations. The Company continues to improve the environment, health and safety (“EHS”)
     management system. During the Reporting Period, the Company revised core safety management
     system documents such as the EHS System Management Manual (             《EHS體系管理手冊》) and the EHS
     Accident/Incident Management Procedure (《EHS事故 ╱ 事件管理規程》) , added the Fire Control Room
     Management Procedure (《消防控制室管理規程》) , and obtained ISO45001 Occupational Health and
     Safety Management System certification.

     Keymed always prioritizes the personal safety of its employees above all its work, implementing full
     lifecycle EHS management that covers factory construction, product research and development, pilot
     testing and commercial production to prevent safety accidents. It is committed to creating a workplace
     with “zero accidents, zero injuries and zero occupational diseases”. The Company has established an
     EHS department which is equipped with a professional EHS management team composed of personnel
     with relevant professional qualifications such as national registered safety engineers. At the same time,
     the Company has established an EHS management committee, with the main responsible person serving
     as the director and heads from various departments serving as committee members. In order to ensure
     the soundness and effectiveness of the operation of the system, the Company regularly conducts internal
     audit and management review of the EHS system, focusing on the compliance of the EHS management
     system, the integrity of the system and the status of production site management. During the Reporting
     Period, the Company held 12 EHS thematic meetings, conducted multiple safety inspections, and
     achieved a rectification rate of 100%.

     The Company has established a dual prevention mechanism, encompassing safety risk tiered control,
     as well as hidden risks investigation and management. We have also refined our risk prevention and
     resolution mechanisms, organized safety risk identification, and implemented targeted risk control
     measures for identified risks. Furthermore, the Company has set up an EHS observation and reporting
     mechanism, allowing employees to promptly report discovered hidden risks through an online reporting
     process. Additionally, we conduct monthly self-inspections of hidden risks across various departments,
     as well as comprehensive company-level inspections.
                                                        2025 ANNUAL REPORT     KEYMED BIOSCIENCES INC.      75




                                         Environmental, Social and Governance Report




In terms of occupational disease prevention and control, Keymed strictly adheres to national occupational
health and safety requirements, establishes a comprehensive occupational disease prevention and
control system and related policies, and implements various measures to carry out occupational disease
prevention and control work, including:

•    Strictly adhere to the “three simultaneities” management requirements for occupational disease
     prevention facilities of construction projects, and conduct risk assessments for toxic and hazardous
     positions in new projects;

•    Provide risk warnings and notifications to employees regarding occupational health, establish
     individual occupational health records for employees, and organize pre-employment, on-the-job,
     and off-the-job occupational health examinations;

•    Post occupational hazard notices and warning signs in the workplace, and provide labor protection
     supplies that meet standards and usage requirements;

•    During the Reporting Period, workplace occupational hazard factor inspections were conducted,
     and the annual declaration of occupational hazard factors was completed;

•    In daily supervision, we conduct inspections on the provision of labor protection supplies and
     supervise their wearing, achieving closed-loop management of occupational health.

In addition, the Company continuously strengthens safety production and occupational health education
and training, incorporating relevant content into the new employee training program. We also conduct
various trainings covering occupational health and safety laws and regulations, first aid knowledge, fire
evacuation, traffic safety, special equipment, chemical safety, etc., achieving a 100% coverage rate for
safety training.

     BENEVOLENT DEEDS NOURISH THE HEART, AND INCLUSIVENESS LEADS TO GREATNESS

     Keymed adheres to a patient-centered approach, actively fulfills its corporate social responsibility, and is
     committed to enhancing the accessibility and affordability of high-quality drugs, thus fulfilling its original
     intention of providing inclusive healthcare through practical actions. It actively participates in social
     welfare, leveraging its professional expertise to convey health care, and contributes to areas including
     rural revitalization and emergency rescue.

     Inclusive Healthcare

     Keymed has always regarded improving the accessibility and affordability of innovative drugs as its
     original business intention and important commitment to patients. It has been deeply engaged in
     addressing unmet clinical needs of patients, developing more globally competitive innovative drugs,
     and benefiting more patients in China and even globally through innovation at source. We contribute to
     improving the overall health level of the population, reducing the burden on patients, and promoting the
     high-quality development of the pharmaceutical industry.

     To enhance the accessibility of drugs, Keymed has partnered with major logistics and transportation
     providers to ensure efficient and stable drug delivery. The Company’s drug supply has reached over
     240 cities, serving more than 50 primary distributors, more than 600 pharmacies, and more than 1,600
     hospitals, fully meeting patients’ medication needs.

      Case: Kangyueda (康悅達 ®) (Stapokibart) successfully included in the National Reimbursement Drug List

      In December 2025, Keymed’s self-developed national Class 1 new drug Kangyueda ( 康悅達 ® )
      (Stapokibart) was successfully included in the National Reimbursement Drug List for Basic Medical
      Insurance, Work-Related Injury Insurance and Maternity Insurance (2025) (《國家基本醫療保險、
      工商保險和生育保險藥品目錄（2025年）》) . As the first domestically developed and approved IL-4Rα
      humanized monoclonal antibody in China, this drug can be used to treat moderate-to-severe atopic
      dermatitis (AD) in adults, chronic rhinosinusitis with nasal polyps (CRSwNP) and seasonal allergic
      rhinitis.

      Before being included in the medical insurance, patients’ copay for Kangyueda (康悅達 ®) (Stapokibart)
      was RMB1,812 per vial, and a promotion policy of “buy two get one free” was offered. Since January 1,
      2026, the medical insurance payment price of Kangyueda (康悅達 ®) (Stapokibart) has been reduced
      to RMB1,039 per vial. This adjustment significantly reduces the burden of medication on patients,
      enabling more patients in need to obtain innovative treatments at an affordable price.

     GREEN DEVELOPMENT, CO-PROTECTION AND COEXISTENCE

     Adhering to the concept of green development, Keymed continuously improves its environmental
     management system, promotes sustainable development with green momentum, scientifically manages
     waste gas, wastewater, and waste, and minimizes environmental footprint; actively promotes resource
     conservation and efficient energy utilization, while closely monitoring climate change risks, actively
     responding to climate change, and working with all parties to build a sustainable and beautiful home.

     Environmental Management

      Keymed has strictly complied with the Environmental Protection Law of the People’s Republic of China
     (《中華人民共和國環境保護法》) , the Atmospheric Pollution Prevention and Control Law of the People’s
      Republic of China (《中華人民共和國大氣污染防治法》) , the Water Pollution Prevention and Control Law
      of the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國水污染防治法》) , the Law of the People’s Republic
      of China on Prevention and Control of Environment Pollution by Solid Waste (《中華人民共和國固體廢物
      污染環境防治法》) and other relevant laws and regulations relating to air and greenhouse gas emissions,
      discharges into water and land, generation of hazardous and nonhazardous waste and reduction
      of significant impacts on the environment and natural resource. The Company has established a
      comprehensive environmental protection management system by formulating management rules such as
      the Environmental Management Procedure (  《環境管理規程》) , the EHS Management Manual (《EHS管理手
      冊》) , and the Environmental Protection Responsibility System (《環保責任制》) . During the Reporting Period,
      the Company continued to promote the standardized construction of the environmental management
      system, newly developed four environmental management documents including the Operating Procedures
      for the Continuous Inactivation System in Workshop N (《N車間連續滅活系統操作規程》) , and revised six
      environmental management documents including the Standard Operating Procedures for the Wastewater
      Treatment System (《污水處理系統標準操作規程》) , further refining environmental protection operating
      procedures to ensure effective implementation of various environmental protection requirements. In
      addition, the Company has obtained ISO14001 environmental management system certification.

     With “protecting the environment and pursuing sustainable development” as its core philosophy, Keymed
     continues to advance the construction of its environmental management system. The Company has
     set up an EHS department, equipped with a professional safety and environmental management team
     led by the nationally certified safety engineers and experts from the Chengdu Occupational Safety and
     Health Association, and allocated part-time security and environmental protection personnel within
     each subsidiary’s workshop to form a “headquarters-subsidiary” hierarchical management model to
     ensure the professionalism and effectiveness of the environmental management work. Through strict
     implementation of the EHS Target Assessment Procedures (       《EHS目標考核規程》) , the Company clearly
     defined the responsibility for safety and environmental protection at all levels, to realize the allocation of
     the responsibility to the positions and supervision to the person.

     The Company sets environmental protection-related goals and regularly reviews their completion status.
     To ensure the achievement of these goals, the Company systematically implements plans and measures
     for pollution prevention, energy conservation, emission reduction, and ecological protection, aiming to
     minimize the negative impact of our operations on the environment. Through practical actions, we strive
     to safeguard the ecological environment.
